    <!-- INTERACTIVE SCRIPTS -->
    <script>
        // 1. Main Hero Banner Controller
        function moveBanner(idx) {
            document.getElementById('main-banner-slider').style.transform = `translateX(-${idx * 50}%)`;
        }
        
        let heroIdx = 0;
        setInterval(() => {
            heroIdx = heroIdx === 0 ? 1 : 0;
            moveBanner(heroIdx);
        }, 6000);

        // 3. Six Card Carousel Logic
        let currentPos = 0;
        function scrollDeals(direction) {
            const slider = document.getElementById('six-card-slider');
            const cardWidth = 324; // Width (300) + Gap (24)
            const maxScroll = -(cardWidth * 3); // 6 cards total, 3 visible on desktop
            
            currentPos += direction * cardWidth;
            
            // Boundary checks
            if (currentPos < maxScroll) currentPos = maxScroll;
            if (currentPos > 0) currentPos = 0;
            
            slider.style.transform = `translateX(${currentPos}px)`;
        }

        // 6. Bottom Banner Auto-play
        let footerIdx = 0;
        setInterval(() => {
            footerIdx = footerIdx === 0 ? 1 : 0;
            document.getElementById('footer-banner-slider').style.transform = `translateX(-${footerIdx * 50}%)`;
        }, 7000);
    </script>
<script>
        let currentSlide = 0;
        const totalSlides = 6;
        const carousel = document.getElementById('hero-carousel');
        const dots = document.querySelectorAll('.dot');

        function updateCarousel() {
            carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${currentSlide * 100}%)`;
            dots.forEach((dot, index) => {
                if(index === currentSlide) {
                    dot.classList.remove('bg-white/50', 'w-2');
                    dot.classList.add('bg-white', 'w-4');
                } else {
                    dot.classList.remove('bg-white', 'w-4');
                    dot.classList.add('bg-white/50', 'w-2');
                }
            });
        }

        function moveSlide(direction) {
            currentSlide = (currentSlide + direction + totalSlides) % totalSlides;
            updateCarousel();
        }

        // Auto play
        setInterval(() => {
            moveSlide(1);
        }, 5000);

        // Dot navigation
        dots.forEach((dot, index) => {
            dot.addEventListener('click', () => {
                currentSlide = index;
                updateCarousel();
            });
        });

        // Initialize
        updateCarousel();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

        // Simple Carousel Logic
        let carouselPos = 0;
        function moveCarousel(direction) {
            const container = document.getElementById('deal-carousel');
            const cardWidth = 324; // Width + gap
            carouselPos += direction;
            if (carouselPos < 0) carouselPos = 0;
            if (carouselPos > 3) carouselPos = 3;
            container.style.transform = `translateX(-${carouselPos * cardWidth}px)`;
        }

        // Auto Slider for Banner
        let bannerIdx = 0;
        setInterval(() => {
            const slider = document.getElementById('banner-slider');
            bannerIdx = (bannerIdx + 1) % 6;
            slider.style.transform = `translateX(-${bannerIdx * 100}%)`;
        }, 4000);
    </script>

<script>
    const fireImages = [
        'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1540541338287-41700207dee6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80',
        'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1520250497591-112f2f40a3f4?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80',
        'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1571896349842-33c89424de2d?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80',
        'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544124499-58912cbddaad?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80',
        'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1566073771259-6a8506099945?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80'
    ];

    const fireCarousel = document.getElementById('fireCarousel');

    // CREATE CARDS
    fireImages.forEach((img, i) => {
        fireCarousel.innerHTML += `
        <div class="min-w-full flex justify-center">
            
            <div class="relative group w-full max-w-4xl h-[500px] rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-gray-200 bg-white">

                <img src="${img}" 
                    class="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105">

                <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 via-black/20 to-transparent"></div>

                <!-- Top Badge -->
                <div class="absolute top-6 left-6">
                    <div class="bg-white/95 px-4 py-2 rounded-full flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg">
                        <span class="animate-pulse text-lg">🔥</span>
                        <span class="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-red-600">
                            Flash Sale Active
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Bottom Content -->
                <div class="absolute bottom-8 left-8 text-white">
                    <p class="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-yellow-400 mb-3">
                        Limited Offer
                    </p>

                    <h3 class="text-4xl font-black mb-3">
                        Amazing Destination ${i + 1}
                    </h3>

                    <p class="text-white/80 max-w-lg">
                        Luxury stay with premium experience and exclusive discounts.
                    </p>

                    <button class="mt-6 bg-white text-black px-6 py-3 rounded-full font-semibold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-all">
                        Explore Now
                    </button>
                </div>

            </div>

        </div>
        `;
    });

    // SLIDER
    let currentIndex = 0;

    function scrollCarousel(direction) {
        const totalCards = fireImages.length;

        currentIndex += direction;

        if (currentIndex < 0) {
            currentIndex = totalCards - 1;
        }

        if (currentIndex >= totalCards) {
            currentIndex = 0;
        }

        fireCarousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${currentIndex * 100}%)`;
    }
</script>

            </p>
        </div>
    </footer>

    <!-- SHARED CAROUSEL SCRIPT -->
    <script>
        function scrollGrid(id, amount) {
            const el = document.getElementById(id);
            el.scrollBy({ left: amount, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
    </script>

<script>
    const carousel = document.getElementById("carousel");
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById("nextBtn");
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById("prevBtn");

    let scrollAmount = 0;

    nextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount += 370;
        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });

    prevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount -= 370;

        if (scrollAmount < 0) {
            scrollAmount = 0;
        }

        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });
</script>

<!-- FAQ SCRIPT -->
<script>
    const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll(".faq-item");

    faqItems.forEach((item) => {
        const btn = item.querySelector(".faq-btn");
        const content = item.querySelector(".faq-content");
        const arrow = item.querySelector(".faq-arrow");

        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {

            content.classList.toggle("hidden");

            arrow.classList.toggle("rotate-180");
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById("toggleBtn");
    const moreText = document.getElementById("moreText");

    toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        moreText.classList.toggle("line-clamp-5");

        if (moreText.classList.contains("line-clamp-5")) {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read More";
        } else {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read Less";
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    const carousel = document.getElementById("carousel");
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById("nextBtn");
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById("prevBtn");

    let scrollAmount = 0;

    nextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount += 370;
        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });

    prevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount -= 370;

        if (scrollAmount < 0) {
            scrollAmount = 0;
        }

        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });
</script>

    const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll(".faq-item");

    faqItems.forEach((item) => {
        const btn = item.querySelector(".faq-btn");
        const content = item.querySelector(".faq-content");
        const arrow = item.querySelector(".faq-arrow");

        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {

            content.classList.toggle("hidden");

            arrow.classList.toggle("rotate-180");
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById("toggleBtn");
    const moreText = document.getElementById("moreText");

    toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        moreText.classList.toggle("line-clamp-5");

        if (moreText.classList.contains("line-clamp-5")) {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read More";
        } else {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read Less";
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    const carousel = document.getElementById("carousel");
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById("nextBtn");
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById("prevBtn");

    let scrollAmount = 0;

    nextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount += 370;
        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });

    prevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount -= 370;

        if (scrollAmount < 0) {
            scrollAmount = 0;
        }

        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });
</script>

<script>
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById("toggleBtn");
    const moreText = document.getElementById("moreText");

    toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        moreText.classList.toggle("line-clamp-5");

        if (moreText.classList.contains("line-clamp-5")) {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read More";
        } else {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read Less";
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    const carousel = document.getElementById("carousel");
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById("nextBtn");
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById("prevBtn");

    let scrollAmount = 0;

    nextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount += 370;
        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });

    prevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount -= 370;

        if (scrollAmount < 0) {
            scrollAmount = 0;
        }

        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });
</script>

<script>
    const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll(".faq-item");

    faqItems.forEach((item) => {
        const btn = item.querySelector(".faq-btn");
        const content = item.querySelector(".faq-content");
        const arrow = item.querySelector(".faq-arrow");

        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {

            content.classList.toggle("hidden");

            arrow.classList.toggle("rotate-180");
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById("toggleBtn");
    const moreText = document.getElementById("moreText");

    toggleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        moreText.classList.toggle("line-clamp-5");

        if (moreText.classList.contains("line-clamp-5")) {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read More";
        } else {
            toggleBtn.innerText = "Read Less";
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    const carousel = document.getElementById("carousel");
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById("nextBtn");
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById("prevBtn");

    let scrollAmount = 0;

    nextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount += 370;
        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });

    prevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount -= 370;

        if (scrollAmount < 0) {
            scrollAmount = 0;
        }

        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });
</script>

<!-- FAQ SCRIPT -->
<script>
    const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll(".faq-item");

    faqItems.forEach((item) => {
        const btn = item.querySelector(".faq-btn");
        const content = item.querySelector(".faq-content");
        const arrow = item.querySelector(".faq-arrow");

        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {

            content.classList.toggle("hidden");

            arrow.classList.toggle("rotate-180");
        });
    });
</script>

<!-- ADD THIS SCRIPT BEFORE </body> -->

<script>
const carousel = document.getElementById('carousel');
const nextBtn = document.getElementById('nextBtn');
const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prevBtn');

nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    carousel.scrollBy({
        left: 350,
        behavior: 'smooth'
    });
});

prevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    carousel.scrollBy({
        left: -350,
        behavior: 'smooth'
    });
});
</script>

<!-- FAQ JSON DATA -->
<script>
const faqData = [
    {
        question: "What is a multi-centre holiday?",
        answer: "A multi-centre holiday allows you to visit two or more destinations in one trip, combining cities, beaches, and luxury resorts into one seamless itinerary."
    },

    {
        question: "Can I customise my itinerary?",
        answer: "Yes, all itineraries are fully customisable including destinations, hotel categories, flights, experiences, and travel durations."
    },

    {
        question: "Are international flights included?",
        answer: "Most luxury packages include international and internal flights along with baggage allowances."
    },

    {
        question: "Do you provide airport transfers?",
        answer: "Yes, airport transfers are included in most itineraries for a smooth and stress-free travel experience."
    },

    {
        question: "Are your holidays ATOL protected?",
        answer: "Yes, qualifying package holidays are fully ATOL protected for financial security and peace of mind."
    },

    {
        question: "Can I upgrade my hotels or flights?",
        answer: "Absolutely. You can upgrade to premium flights, luxury suites, private villas, and exclusive resorts."
    },

    {
        question: "Do you offer honeymoon packages?",
        answer: "Yes, we specialise in luxury honeymoon escapes with romantic experiences and premium resorts."
    },

    {
        question: "How do I request a custom quote?",
        answer: "You can contact our travel experts or submit an enquiry form to receive a personalised quote."
    }
];



/* RENDER FAQ FRONTEND */
const faqContainer = document.getElementById("faqContainer");

faqData.forEach((faq, index) => {

    faqContainer.innerHTML += `
    
    <div class="faq-item bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
        
        <button class="faq-btn w-full flex items-center justify-between p-6 text-left">

            <span class="font-semibold text-lg">
                ${faq.question}
            </span>

            <span class="faq-icon text-2xl transition-all duration-300">
                ^
            </span>

        </button>

        <div class="faq-content hidden px-6 pb-6 text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
            ${faq.answer}
        </div>

    </div>
    
    `;
});



/* FAQ TOGGLE */
const faqButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.faq-btn');

faqButtons.forEach(button => {

    button.addEventListener('click', () => {

        const faqItem = button.parentElement;
        const content = faqItem.querySelector('.faq-content');
        const icon = faqItem.querySelector('.faq-icon');

        // Close all others
        document.querySelectorAll('.faq-content').forEach(item => {
            if(item !== content){
                item.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('.faq-icon').forEach(item => {
            if(item !== icon){
                item.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
            }
        });

        // Toggle current
        content.classList.toggle('hidden');

        if(content.classList.contains('hidden')){
            icon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
        } else {
            icon.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)';
        }

    });

});
</script>

<script>
function toggleReadMore() {
    const moreText = document.getElementById("moreText");
    const btn = document.getElementById("readBtn");

    if (moreText.style.display === "none") {
        moreText.style.display = "block";
        btn.innerText = "Read Less";
    } else {
        moreText.style.display = "none";
        btn.innerText = "Read More";
    }
}

const images = [
    "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1507525428034-b723cf961d3e?q=80&w=2000",
    "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1493558103817-58b2924bce98?q=80&w=2000",
    "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1500530855697-b586d89ba3ee?q=80&w=2000",
    "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1519046904884-53103b34b206?q=80&w=2000"
];

let current = 0;

setInterval(() => {
    current = (current + 1) % images.length;
    document.getElementById("bannerImage").src = images[current];
}, 4000);
</script>

<script>
function toggleReadMore() {
    const moreText = document.getElementById("moreText");
    const btn = document.getElementById("readBtn");

    if (moreText.classList.contains("hidden")) {
        moreText.classList.remove("hidden");
        btn.innerText = "Read Less";
    } else {
        moreText.classList.add("hidden");
        btn.innerText = "Read More";
    }
}
</script>

<!-- HIDE SCROLLBAR -->
<style>
#holidayScroll::-webkit-scrollbar{
    display:none;
}
</style>

<!-- DRAG SCROLL SCRIPT -->
<script>
const slider = document.getElementById('holidayScroll');

let isDown = false;
let startX;
let scrollLeft;

slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    isDown = true;
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
    startX = e.pageX - slider.offsetLeft;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;
});

slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.style.cursor = 'grab';
});

slider.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.style.cursor = 'grab';
});

slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if(!isDown) return;

    e.preventDefault();

    const x = e.pageX - slider.offsetLeft;
    const walk = (x - startX) * 2;

    slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - walk;
});

slider.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    slider.scrollLeft += e.deltaY;
});
</script>

<!-- HOLIDAY STYLES -->
<script>
function showDealCategory(category, el){

    const cards = document.querySelectorAll(".deal-card");
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll(".deal-btn");

    cards.forEach(card => {
        if(card.classList.contains(category)){
            card.style.display = "block";
        } else {
            card.style.display = "none";
        }
    });

    buttons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.style.background = "white";
        btn.style.color = "#0f172a";
    });

    el.style.background = "#0f172a";
    el.style.color = "white";
}

function scrollDeals(direction){
    const container = document.getElementById("dealsCarousel");

    container.scrollBy({
        left: direction * 400,
        behavior: "smooth"
    });
}

function showDestination(category, el){

    const cards = document.querySelectorAll(".destination-card");
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll(".destination-btn");

    cards.forEach(card => {
        if(card.classList.contains(category)){
            card.style.display = "flex";
        } else {
            card.style.display = "none";
        }
    });

    buttons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.style.background = "white";
        btn.style.color = "#0f172a";
    });

    el.style.background = "#0f172a";
    el.style.color = "white";
}
</script>

<script>
function showDestination(category, btn) {
  const allCards = document.querySelectorAll('.destination-card');
  allCards.forEach(c => {
    c.style.display = c.classList.contains(category) ? 'flex' : 'none';
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.destination-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
}

// Drag scroll
const slider = document.getElementById('cards-container');
let isDown = false;
let startX, scrollLeft;
slider.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
  isDown = true;
  slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  startX = e.pageX - slider.offsetLeft;
  scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;
});
slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { isDown = false; slider.style.cursor='grab'; });
slider.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { isDown = false; slider.style.cursor='grab'; });
slider.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
  if(!isDown) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const x = e.pageX - slider.offsetLeft;
  const walk = (x - startX) * 2;
  slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - walk;
});
</script>

<script>
function toggleReadMore() {
    const extraText = document.getElementById('extra-text');
    const button = document.getElementById('read-toggle');
    if (extraText.style.display === 'none') {
        extraText.style.display = 'block';
        button.textContent = 'Read Less';
    } else {
        extraText.style.display = 'none';
        button.textContent = 'Read More';
    }
}
</script>

        What to Expect from Croatia Weather
    </h2>

    <p style="max-width: 900px; margin: 0 auto 50px auto; color: #666; font-size: 1.1rem; line-height: 1.8;">
        Croatia enjoys a pleasant Mediterranean climate along its coastline, with warm summers and gently changing seasons.
        Spring brings mild temperatures and bright days, ideal for sightseeing and coastal walks. Summer is hot and sunny
        with refreshing sea breezes, while autumn remains comfortably warm and quieter. Winter is mild along the coast,
        offering crisp evenings and clear, calm days.

<script>
    const carousel = document.getElementById("carousel");
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById("nextBtn");
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById("prevBtn");

    let scrollAmount = 0;

    nextBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount += 370;
        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });

    prevBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        scrollAmount -= 370;

        if (scrollAmount < 0) {
            scrollAmount = 0;
        }

        carousel.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollAmount}px)`;
    });
</script>

<script>
function showDestination(category, btn) {
  const allCards = document.querySelectorAll('.destination-card');
  allCards.forEach(c => {
    c.style.display = c.classList.contains(category) ? 'flex' : 'none';
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.destination-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
}

// Drag scroll
const slider = document.getElementById('cards-container');
let isDown = false;
let startX, scrollLeft;
slider.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
  isDown = true;
  slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  startX = e.pageX - slider.offsetLeft;
  scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;
});
slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { isDown = false; slider.style.cursor='grab'; });
slider.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { isDown = false; slider.style.cursor='grab'; });
slider.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
  if(!isDown) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const x = e.pageX - slider.offsetLeft;
  const walk = (x - startX) * 2;
  slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - walk;
});
</script>

<script>
function showDealCategory(category, el){

    const cards = document.querySelectorAll(".deal-card");
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll(".deal-btn");

    cards.forEach(card => {
        if(card.classList.contains(category)){
            card.style.display = "block";
        } else {
            card.style.display = "none";
        }
    });

    buttons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.style.background = "white";
        btn.style.color = "#0f172a";
    });

    el.style.background = "#0f172a";
    el.style.color = "white";
}

function scrollDeals(direction){
    const container = document.getElementById("dealsCarousel");

    container.scrollBy({
        left: direction * 400,
        behavior: "smooth"
    });
}

function showDestination(category, el){

    const cards = document.querySelectorAll(".destination-card");
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll(".destination-btn");

    cards.forEach(card => {
        if(card.classList.contains(category)){
            card.style.display = "flex";
        } else {
            card.style.display = "none";
        }
    });

    buttons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.style.background = "white";
        btn.style.color = "#0f172a";
    });

    el.style.background = "#0f172a";
    el.style.color = "white";
}
</script>

<!-- TAB SCRIPT -->
<script>

function showTab(tabId){

  document.querySelectorAll('.tabContent').forEach(tab=>{
    tab.classList.add('hidden');
  });

  document.getElementById(tabId).classList.remove('hidden');

  document.querySelectorAll('.tabBtn').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.classList.remove('bg-[#CB2187]');
    btn.classList.remove('text-white');
    btn.classList.add('bg-gray-100');
  });

  event.target.classList.remove('bg-gray-100');
  event.target.classList.add('bg-[#CB2187]');
  event.target.classList.add('text-white');

}

</script>
